{\r\n      clearInput();\r\n      previousTime = null;\r\n      if (!state || state.phase === \"finished\") return;\r\n      bike.pause(state);\r\n      pauseButton.textContent = \"Pokračovať\";\r\n      if (exitPending) return;\r\n      panel.hidden = false;\r\n      panel.replaceChildren();\r\n      heading(\"Jazda je pozastavená\");\r\n      const resume = button(\"Pokračovať v jazde\", label);\r\n      control.type = \"button\";\r\n      control.style.touchAction = \"none\";\r\n      control.style.userSelect = \"none\";\r\n      control.setAttribute(\"aria-label\", value ===      event.preventDefault();\r\n        if (!state || ![\"riding\", \"countdown\", \"fallen\"].includes(state.phase) || exitPending || pointers.has(event.pointerId)) return;\r\n });\r\n      const release = event =\u003e {\r\n        pointers.delete(event.pointerId);\r\n        try { if (control.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) roadCenter(vertical, distance) {\r\n      const depth = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (vertical - horizon) / (riderY - horizon)));\r\n      return 480 + Math.sin(distance / 175 + (1 - depth) * 2.8) * 88 * (1 - depth);\r\n    }\r\n    function villageBlend(distance) {\r\n      const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (distance - 945) / 220));\r\n      return progress * progress * (3 - 2 * progress);\r\n    }\r\n    function roadHalf(vertical, distance) {\r\n      const blend = villageBlend(distance);\r\n      return 5 + 8 * blend + Math.max(0, (vertical - horizon) / (riderY - horizon)) * (105 + 195 * blend);\r\n    }\r\n    function project(delta, lateral, distance) {\r\n      const depth = 1 - delta / 130;\r\n      const vertical = horizon + (riderY - horizon) * depth * depth;\r\n      return { x: roadCenter(vertical, distance) + lateral * roadHalf(vertical, distance), y: vertical, scale: .13 + .87 * depth * depth };\r\n    }\r\n    function rectangle(left, top, width, height, color) {\r\n      context.fillStyle = color;\r\n      context.fillRect(Math.round(left), Math.round(top), Math.ceil(width), Math.ceil(height));\r\n    }\r\n    function polygon(points, color) {\r\n (!flags.transport ? \"Dohodni s tatom odvoz miešačky.\" : \"Zastav sa za lesníkom a skontroluj opravu žliabka.\") : state.inventory.includes(\"sparePart\") ? \"Odnes náhradný diel Miešačke.\" : !flags.partAvailable ? \"Opýtaj sa sesternice alebo jej manžela na náhradný diel.\" : !flags.dogSafe ? \"Popros manžela, aby zaistil psa.\" : \"Vezmi náhradný diel z dielne.\" }\r\n    ].filter(quest activeInventory(state) {\r\n    const flags = state.flags;\r\n    const obsolete = {\r\n      chalk: flags.tracks,\r\n      key: flags.pubOpen,\r\n      math: flags.journalFound,\r\n      journal: route: flags.brnoReady,\r\n      sparePart: flags.equipmentReturned\r\n    };\r\n    return state.inventory.filter(item =\u003e !obsolete[item]);\r\n  }\r\n  const api = { places, people, items, oscillator.stop(onset + 1);\r\n        });\r\n      });\r\n    } catch {\r\n      speak({ text: \"Zvuk sa nepodarilo spustiť. Skontroluj zvuk prehliadača a skús to znova.\" });\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  function playRecording() { return playPianoNotes([\"E\", \"G\", \"A\"]); }\r\n  function speak(result) {\r\n    byId(\"speaker\").textContent = result.speaker || \"NG\";\r\n    const names = [\"NG\", ...Object.values(game.people).map(person =\u003e person.name), \"Sesternica\", \"Manžel\", \"Učiteľka\"];\r\n    const turns = result.text.split(new RegExp(`\\\\s+(?=(?:${names.join(\"|\")}):)`, \"u\"));\r\n    const speech = byId(\"speech\");\r\n    speech.replaceChildren();\r\n    turns.forEach((turn, index) =\u003e {\r\n      if (index) speech.append(document.createElement(\"br\"));\r\n      speech.append(document.createTextNode(turn));\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n  function openModal(title, build) { cancelApproach(); byId(\"modal-title\").textContent = title; byId(\"modal-content\").replaceChildren(); build(byId(\"modal-content\")); if (!modal.open) modal.showModal(); renderHeldItem(); }\r\n  function closeModal() { stopMusic(); modal.close(); }\r\n  modal.addEventListener(\"close\", stopMusic);\r\n  function showIntro() {\r\n    const opening = game.createState(); opening.location = \"pub\";\r\n    globalThis.ZubakArt.draw(opening, false, 0, player);\r\n    byId(\"intro\").style.backgroundImage = `url(\"${byId(\"scene\").toDataURL(\"image/png\")}\")`;\r\n    byId(\"intro-continue\").hidden = state.finished;\r\n    byId(\"intro-finale\").hidden = !state.finished;\r\n    byId(\"intro\").showModal();\r\n  }\r\n  function startStory() {\r\n    state = game.createState();\r\n    state.flags.introSeen = true;\r\n    byId(\"intro\").close();\r\n    travel(\"home\");\r\n    speak({ speaker: \"NG\", text: \"Najprv zistím, čo vie mama, a pozriem sa na studňu v lese za domom. state.finished;\r\n    const previousInventory = new Set(state.inventory);\r\n    const previousClues = state.clues.length;\r\n    const wasOpen = Boolean(state.flags.pubOpen);\r\n    const wasFixed = Boolean(state.flags.waterFixed);\r\n    const wasWellOpen = Boolean(state.flags.wellOpen);\r\n    const milestones = () =\u003e [\r\n      ...game.quests(state),\r\n      { title: \"Záhada miznúcej vody\", done: Boolean(state.flags.waterFixed) }\r\n    ];\r\n    const previouslyDone = new Set(milestones().filter(quest =\u003e quest.done).map(quest =\u003e quest.title));\r\n    const result = game.interact(state, action);\r\n    const completed = milestones().filter(quest =\u003e quest.done \u0026\u0026 !previouslyDone.has(quest.title));\r\n    if (action.type === \"object\" \u0026\u0026 action.target === \"bear\") playEffect(\"bear\");\r\n    else if (action.type === \"object\" \u0026\u0026 action.target === \"suv\") playEffect(\"suv\");\r\n    else if (action.type === \"object\" \u0026\u0026 action.target === \"hatch\" \u0026\u0026 !wasWellOpen \u0026\u0026 state.flags.wellOpen) playEffect(\"hatch\");\r\n    else if (!wasFixed \u0026\u0026 state.flags.waterFixed) playEffect(\"repair\");\r\n    else if (!wasOpen \u0026\u0026 state.flags.pubOpen) playEffect(\"lockOpen\");\r\n    else if (action.type === \"object\" \u0026\u0026 action.target === \"door\" \u0026\u0026 action.answer \u0026\u0026 !state.flags.pubOpen \u0026\u0026 state.flags.pubCodeKnown \u0026\u0026 previousInventory.has(\"math\")) playEffect(\"lockWrong\");\r\n    else if (completed.length || (!wasFinished \u0026\u0026 state.finished)) playEffect(\"quest\");\r\n    else if (state.inventory.some(item =\u003e !previousInventory.has(item))) playEffect(\"item\");\r\n    else if (state.clues.length \u003e previousClues) playEffect(\"clue\");\r\n    speak(result); save(); render();\r\n    if (!wasFinished \u0026\u0026 state.finished) {\r\n      stopMusic(); (completed.length) {\r\n      openModal(\"Záhada vyriešená!\", content =\u003e {\r\n        content.append(paragraph(\"HOTOVO\", \"muted\"));\r\n        completed.forEach(quest =\u003e }\r\n  function travel(id) {\r\n    stopMusic();\r\n    cancelApproach();\r\n    activePerson = null;\r\n    kettleSelected = false;\r\n    const result = execute({ type: \"travel\", target: id });\r\n    if (state.location === id) { mapVisible = false; selectedItem = null; const entry = game.scene(state).entry || { x: 560, y: 476 }; player.x = entry.x; player.y = entry.y; player.targetX = player.targetY = player.y; player.walking = false;\r\n  }\r\n  function approach(id, interaction) {\r\n    if (game.scene(state).locked) {\r\n      cancelApproach();\r\n      if (!mapVisible \u0026\u0026 id === \"warden\") interaction();\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    const target = [...byId(\"hotspots\").children].find(element =\u003e element.dataset.target === id);\r\n    if (!target || mapVisible) return;\r\n    const bounds = byId(\"hotspots\").getBoundingClientRect();\r\n    const targetBounds = target.getBoundingClientRect();\r\n    player.targetX = Math.max(50, Math.min(910,  pendingInteraction = interaction;\r\n  }\r\n  function showPerson(id, arrived = false) {\r\n    if (!arrived) return approach(id, () =\u003e showPerson(id, true));\r\n    if (id === \"michacka\" selectedItem = null;\r\n      activePerson = id;\r\n      execute({ type: \"talk\", target: id, topic: \"loan\" });\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    if (id === \"ondrej\" \u0026\u0026 selectedItem ===         "content":  "(function (root) {\r\n  \"use strict\";\r\n  const stages = [\r\n    { name: \"Cibuľový základ\", ingredient: \"onion\", action: \"Nakrájať\", seconds: 30 },\r\n    { name: \"Opiecť mäso\", ingredient: \"meat\", action: \"Opiecť\", seconds: 30 },\r\n    { name: \"Dusiť a miešať\", ingredient: null, action: \"Zamiešať\", seconds: 30 },\r\n    { name: \"Dochutiť guláš\", ingredient: \"paprika\", action: \"Dochutiť\", seconds: 30 }\r\n  ];\r\n  function create(name) {\r\n    return { name, phase: \"cooking\", stage: 0, stageTime: 0, elapsed: 0, hits: 0, points: 0,\r\n      mistakes: 0, ingredient: false, lastAction: -1, heat: 50, temperature: 50, feedback: \"\" };\r\n  }\r\n  function marker(state) { return (1 - Math.cos(state.stageTime * Math.PI * 1.26)) / 2; Skús export v samostatnom okne Chrome alebo Edge.\";\r\n            return;\r\n          }\r\n          if (videoUrls[exporting]) URL.revokeObjectURL(videoUrls[exporting]);\r\n          videoUrls[exporting] = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\r\n          const link = document.getElementById(exporting === \"mp4\" ? \"download\" : \"download-webm\");\r\n          link.href = videoUrls[exporting];\r\n          link.download = `zubak-trailer.${exporting}`;\r\n          link.hidden = false;\r\n          status.textContent = `${exporting.toUpperCase()} pripravené (${(blob.size / 1048576).toFixed(1)} MB)`;\r\n        };\r\n        recorder.start();\r\n      }\r\n      const started = performance.now();\r\n      function frame(now) {\r\n        const time = Math.min(duration, (now - started) / 1000);\r\n        draw(time);\r\n        status.textContent = `${exporting ? \"Nahrávam video · \" : \"\"}00:${String(Math.floor(time)).padStart(2, \"0\")} / 00:30`;\r\n        if (time \u003c duration \u0026\u0026 running) animation = requestAnimationFrame(frame);\r\n        else { draw(29.5); finish(); }\r\n      }\r\n      animation = requestAnimationFrame(frame);\r\n    } catch (error) {\r\n      finish();\r\n      status.textContent = `Nepodarilo sa spustiť trailer: ${error.message}`;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  playButton.addEventListener(\"click\", () =\u003e start(false));\r\n  exportButton.addEventListener(\"click\", () =\u003e start(\"mp4\"));\r\n  webmButton.addEventListener(\"click\", () =\u003e start(\"webm\"));\r\n  draw(1);\r\n})();"
